 }
 
+profile_boot_id() {
+	case "$1" in
+	'' | unknown | *[!A-Za-z0-9-]*) return 1 ;;
+	esac
+	[ "${#1}" -le 64 ] || return 1
+	printf '%s' "$1"
+}
+
+box_boot_id() {
+	local boot="${SAND_BOX_BOOT_ID:-}"
+	if [ -z "${boot}" ]; then
+		read -r boot 2>/dev/null <"${SAND_BOX_BOOT_ID_FILE:-/tmp/sand-box-boot-id}" || true
+		boot="$(profile_boot_id "${boot}")" || boot=""
+	fi
+	printf '%s' "${boot:-${SAND_BOX_BOOT_STARTED_AT_MS:-unknown}}"
+}
+
+write_profile_file() {
+	printf '%s\n' "$2" 2>/dev/null >"$1.$$" && mv -f "$1.$$" "$1" 2>/dev/null || rm -f "$1.$$" 2>/dev/null || true
+}
+
+# Runs before next_launch_attempt so the launch count still names the boot
+# that last launched this profile.
+record_profile_origin() {
+	local boot launched_boot="" prior_boot="" prior_recorded="" prior_profile="" recorded="-" late_gate="" gate=0
+	local marker="${CHROME_PROFILE}/.sand-profile-origin.json" origin=unknown created="" boot_field launcher="" writer_boot=""
+	[ -e "${SAND_BOX_WALL_EVIDENCE_RECORDERS_FILE:-/tmp/sand-box-wall-evidence-recorders}" ] && gate=1
+	[ "${gate}" -eq 0 ] || write_profile_file "${BOX_PROFILE_DIR_FILE}" "${CHROME_PROFILE}"
+	boot="$(profile_boot_id "$1")" || return 0
+	read -r launched_boot _ 2>/dev/null <"${CHROME_PROFILE}/.sand-launch-count" || true
+	if [ "${launched_boot}" = "${boot}" ]; then
+		late_gate=',"lateGate":true'
+		read -r prior_boot prior_recorded prior_profile 2>/dev/null <"${BOX_PROFILE_PRIOR_BOOT_FILE}" || true
+		if [ "${prior_boot}" = "${boot}" ] && [ "${prior_profile}" = "${CHROME_PROFILE}" ]; then
+			recorded="$(profile_boot_id "${prior_recorded}")" || recorded="-"
+		fi
+	else
+		recorded="$(profile_boot_id "${launched_boot}")" || recorded="-"
+		write_profile_file "${BOX_PROFILE_PRIOR_BOOT_FILE}" "${boot} ${recorded} ${CHROME_PROFILE}"
+	fi
+	[ "${gate}" -eq 1 ] || return 0
+	[ ! -e "${marker}" ] && [ ! -L "${marker}" ] || return 0
+	# Chrome writes Local State on its first run, so a profile without it or a
+	# launch count has never been opened; any other profile predates the marker.
+	if [ ! -e "${CHROME_PROFILE}/Local State" ] && [ ! -e "${CHROME_PROFILE}/.sand-launch-count" ]; then
+		origin=fresh
+		[ "${BOX_DISPLAY_NUM:-1}" -ge 2 ] 2>/dev/null && origin=fork
+		created="\"createdMs\":$(date +%s%3N),"
+		boot_field="\"${boot}\""
+	elif [ "${recorded}" = "-" ]; then
+		boot_field=null
+		writer_boot=",\"writerBootId\":\"${boot}\""
+	else
+		boot_field="\"${recorded}\""
+	fi
+	case "${SAND_BOX_CHROME_LAUNCHER_SOURCE:-}" in
+	bundle | image) launcher=",\"launcherSource\":\"${SAND_BOX_CHROME_LAUNCHER_SOURCE}\"" ;;
+	esac
+	write_profile_file "${marker}" \
+		"{${created}\"origin\":\"${origin}\",\"bootId\":${boot_field},\"writerVersion\":${BOX_WALL_EVIDENCE_WRITER_VERSION}${launcher}${late_gate}${writer_boot}}"
+}
+
 start_chrome() {
-	local mode="$1" visible=1 started_ms attempt outcome duration_ms cdp_ready_ms launcher_fields shown
+	local mode="$1" visible=1 started_ms attempt outcome duration_ms cdp_ready_ms launcher_fields shown boot
 	shift
 	[ "${mode}" = window ] || visible=0
-	attempt="$(next_launch_attempt)"

 	started_ms="$(date +%s%3N)"
 	if [ "${CHROME_PORT_HELD}" -eq 0 ]; then
 		if [ "${policy_status:-0}" = 3 ]; then

+export function serializeSeed(cookies, now = Date.now(), lineage = undefined) {
+  return JSON.stringify({
+    version: SEED_VERSION,
+    savedAt: now,
+    ...(lineage === undefined ? {} : { firstSavedAt: lineage.firstSavedAt }),
+    ...(lineage?.firstSavedAtFloor === true ? { firstSavedAtFloor: true } : {}),
+    cookies,
+  });
+}
+
+function epochMs(value) {
+  return Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value >= 0 ? value : undefined;
+}
+
+function seedLineage(parsed) {
+  const firstSavedAt = epochMs(parsed.firstSavedAt);
+  if (firstSavedAt !== undefined) {
+    return parsed.firstSavedAtFloor === true
+      ? { firstSavedAt, firstSavedAtFloor: true }
+      : { firstSavedAt };
+  }
+  const savedAt = epochMs(parsed.savedAt);
+  return savedAt === undefined ? undefined : { firstSavedAt: savedAt, firstSavedAtFloor: true };
+}
+
+export function nextSeedLineage(seed, now) {
+  if (seed.kind === "absent") return { firstSavedAt: now };
+  return seed.kind === "ok" && seed.lineage !== undefined
+    ? seed.lineage
+    : { firstSavedAt: now, firstSavedAtFloor: true };
+}
+
+export const isWallEvidenceGateOn = () => existsSync(BOX_WALL_EVIDENCE_RECORDERS_MARKER_PATH);
+
+export function seedLineageToWrite(seed, now, gateOn) {
+  if (gateOn) return nextSeedLineage(seed, now);
+  return seed.kind === "ok" && seed.lineageStored === true ? seed.lineage : undefined;
+}
+
+function seedWriteLineage(deps, seed, now) {
+  return seedLineageToWrite(seed, now, deps.wallEvidence?.gateOn() === true);
 }
 
 export function parseSeed(text) {

+export function cookieFactsFromJar(cookies, observedAtMs) {
+  const facts = Object.fromEntries(
+    WALL_EVIDENCE_COOKIE_FACT_DOMAINS.map((domain) => [
+      domain,
+      { count: 0, flags: 0, observedAtMs },

+      if (!hostMatchesCookieDomain(cookie.domain, domain)) continue;
+      const fact = facts[domain];
+      fact.count = Math.min(fact.count + 1, WALL_EVIDENCE_COOKIE_COUNT_MAX);
+      if (flag >= 0) fact.flags |= 1 << flag;
+    }
+  }
+  return facts;
+}

+export function serializeSeed(cookies, now = Date.now(), lineage = undefined) {
+  return JSON.stringify({
+    version: SEED_VERSION,
+    savedAt: now,
+    ...(lineage === undefined ? {} : { firstSavedAt: lineage.firstSavedAt }),
+    ...(lineage?.firstSavedAtFloor === true ? { firstSavedAtFloor: true } : {}),
+    cookies,
+  });

+
+function epochMs(value) {
+  return Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value >= 0 ? value : undefined;
+}
+
+function seedLineage(parsed) {
+  const firstSavedAt = epochMs(parsed.firstSavedAt);
+  if (firstSavedAt !== undefined) {
+    return parsed.firstSavedAtFloor === true
+      ? { firstSavedAt, firstSavedAtFloor: true }
+      : { firstSavedAt };
+  }
+  const savedAt = epochMs(parsed.savedAt);
+  return savedAt === undefined ? undefined : { firstSavedAt: savedAt, firstSavedAtFloor: true };
+}
+
+export function nextSeedLineage(seed, now) {
+  if (seed.kind === "absent") return { firstSavedAt: now };
+  return seed.kind === "ok" && seed.lineage !== undefined
+    ? seed.lineage
+    : { firstSavedAt: now, firstSavedAtFloor: true };
+}
+
+export const isWallEvidenceGateOn = () => existsSync(BOX_WALL_EVIDENCE_RECORDERS_MARKER_PATH);

+  return seed.kind === "ok" && seed.lineageStored === true ? seed.lineage : undefined;
+}

 // Chrome keeps an in-memory list of all cookies and reads the backing store only until it has fetched them all (https://chromium.googlesource.com/chromium/src/+/refs/tags/151.0.7922.169/net/cookies/cookie_monster.h), and the DevTools IndexedDB domain has no method that writes an entry (https://chromedevtools.github.io/devtools-protocol/tot/IndexedDB/).
+export function cookieFactsFromJar(cookies, observedAtMs) {
+  const facts = Object.fromEntries(
+    WALL_EVIDENCE_COOKIE_FACT_DOMAINS.map((domain) => [
+      domain,
+      { count: 0, flags: 0, observedAtMs },
+    ]),
+  );
+  for (const cookie of cookies) {
+    const flag = WALL_EVIDENCE_COOKIE_FLAGS.indexOf(cookie.name);
+    for (const domain of WALL_EVIDENCE_COOKIE_FACT_DOMAINS) {
+      if (!hostMatchesCookieDomain(cookie.domain, domain)) continue;
+      const fact = facts[domain];
+      fact.count = Math.min(fact.count + 1, WALL_EVIDENCE_COOKIE_COUNT_MAX);
+      if (flag >= 0) fact.flags |= 1 << flag;
+    }
+  }
+  return facts;
+}
